[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2c173270429b8ec6b75768a830961c534a59233e
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..320bbbd1585ee13f76f1f2253ba91b919bc4e568
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/renovate.json
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,6 @@
[codex] +{
[codex] +  "$schema": "https://docs.renovatebot.com/renovate-schema.json",
[codex] +  "extends": [
[codex] +    "github>budgetanalyzer/orchestration//renovate-presets/default"
[codex] +  ]
[codex] +}

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then

[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex]
