[codex]     description: |
[codex]       Set to 'true' to automatically publish build results as a Build Scan on scans.gradle.com.
[codex]       For publication to succeed without user input, you must also provide values for `build-scan-terms-of-use-url` and 'build-scan-terms-of-use-agree'.
[codex]     required: false
[codex]     default: false
[codex] 
[codex]   build-scan-terms-of-use-url:
[codex]     description: The URL to the Build Scan® terms of use. This input must be set to 'https://gradle.com/terms-of-service' or 'https://gradle.com/help/legal-terms-of-use'.
[codex]     required: false
[codex] 
[codex]   build-scan-terms-of-use-agree:
[codex]     description: Indicate that you agree to the Build Scan® terms of use. This input value must be "yes".
[codex]     required: false
[codex] 
[codex]   develocity-access-key:
[codex]     description: Develocity access key. Should be set to a secret containing the Develocity Access key.
[codex]     required: false
[codex] 
[codex]   develocity-token-expiry:
[codex]     description: The Develocity short-lived access tokens expiry in hours. Default is 2 hours.
[codex]     required: false
[codex] 
[codex]   # Wrapper validation configuration
[codex]   validate-wrappers:
[codex]     description: |
[codex]       When 'true' the action will automatically validate all wrapper jars found in the repository.
[codex]       If the wrapper checksums are not valid, the action will fail.
[codex]     required: false
[codex]     default: false
[codex] 
[codex]   allow-snapshot-wrappers:
[codex]     description: |
[codex]       When 'true', wrapper validation will include the checksums of snapshot wrapper jars. 
[codex]       Use this if you are running with nightly or snapshot versions of the Gradle wrapper.
[codex]     required: false
[codex]     default: false
[codex] 
[codex]   # DEPRECATED ACTION INPUTS
[codex] 
[codex]   # EXPERIMENTAL ACTION INPUTS
[codex]   # The following action properties allow fine-grained tweaking of the action caching behaviour.
[codex]   # These properties are experimental and not (yet) designed for production use, and may change without notice in a subsequent release of `setup-gradle`.
[codex]   # Use at your own risk!
[codex]   gradle-home-cache-strict-match:
[codex]     description: When 'true', the action will not attempt to restore the Gradle User Home entries from other Jobs.
[codex]     required: false
[codex]     default: false
[codex] 
[codex]   # INTERNAL ACTION INPUTS
[codex]   # These inputs should not be configured directly, and are only used to pass environmental information to the action
[codex]   workflow-job-context:
[codex]     description: Used to uniquely identify the current job invocation. Defaults to the matrix values for this job; this should not be overridden by users (INTERNAL).
[codex]     required: false
[codex]     default: ${{ toJSON(matrix) }}
[codex] 
[codex]   github-token:
[codex]     description: The GitHub token used to authenticate when submitting via the Dependency Submission API.
[codex]     default: ${{ github.token }}
[codex]     required: false
[codex] 
[codex] outputs:
[codex]   build-scan-url:
[codex]     description: Link to the Build Scan® generated by a Gradle build. Note that this output applies to a Step executing Gradle, not to the `setup-gradle` Step itself.
[codex]   dependency-graph-file:
[codex]     description: Path to the GitHub Dependency Graph snapshot file generated by a Gradle build. Note that this output applies to a Step executing Gradle, not to the `setup-gradle` Step itself.
[codex]   gradle-version:
[codex]     description: Version of Gradle that was setup by the action
[codex] 
[codex] runs:
[codex]   using: 'node24'
[codex]   main: '../dist/dependency-submission/main/index.js'
[codex]   post: '../dist/dependency-submission/post/index.js'
[codex] 
[codex] branding:
[codex]   icon: 'box'
[codex]   color: 'gray-dark'
[codex] # The `dependency-submission` action
[codex] 
[codex] The `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action provides the simplest (and recommended) way to generate a 
[codex] dependency graph for your project. This action will attempt to detect all dependencies used by your build
[codex] without building and testing the project itself.
[codex] 
[codex] The dependency graph snapshot is generated via integration with the [GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin](https://plugins.gradle.org/plugin/org.gradle.github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin), and submitted to your repository via the 
[codex] [GitHub Dependency Submission API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/dependency-graph/dependency-submission).
[codex] The generated snapshot files can be submitted in the same job, or saved for submission in a subsequent job.
[codex] 
[codex] The generated dependency graph includes all of the dependencies in your build, and is used by GitHub to generate 
[codex] [Dependabot Alerts](https://docs.github.com/en/code-security/dependabot/dependabot-alerts/about-dependabot-alerts) 
[codex] for vulnerable dependencies, as well as to populate the 
[codex] [Dependency Graph insights view](https://docs.github.com/en/code-security/supply-chain-security/understanding-your-software-supply-chain/exploring-the-dependencies-of-a-repository#viewing-the-dependency-graph).
[codex] 
[codex] If you're confused by the behaviour you're seeing or have specific questions, please check out [the FAQ](dependency-submission-faq.md) before raising an issue.
[codex] 
[codex] > [!NOTE]
[codex] > ### ⚡️ Choice of caching providers in v6
[codex] > To provide the fastest possible build experience this action includes **Enhanced Caching** via `gradle-actions-caching`, an optimized provider powered by proprietary technology. This feature is **free for all public repositories** and is currently available as a **Free Preview** for private repositories. 
[codex] >
[codex] > **Prefer a 100% Open Source (MIT) path?**
[codex] > We also provide a **Basic Caching** provider as a thin wrapper over `actions/cache`. This provider is **free for all repositories** (public and private) and can be enabled at any time by setting `cache-provider: basic`.
[codex] >
[codex] > For a full breakdown of the components, usage tiers, and our **Safe Harbor** data privacy commitment, see our [Distribution & Licensing Guide](../DISTRIBUTION.md).
[codex] 
[codex] ## General usage
[codex] 
[codex] The following workflow will generate a dependency graph for a Gradle project and submit it immediately to the repository via the
[codex] Dependency Submission API. For most projects, this default configuration should be all that you need.
[codex] 
[codex] Simply add this as a new workflow file to your repository (eg `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`).
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] name: Dependency Submission
[codex] 
[codex] on:
[codex]   push:
[codex]     branches: [ 'main' ]
[codex] 
[codex] permissions:
[codex]   contents: write
[codex] 
[codex] jobs:
[codex]   dependency-submission:
[codex]     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex]     steps:
[codex]     - uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex]     - uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex]       with:
[codex]         distribution: temurin
[codex]         java-version: 17
[codex] 
[codex]     - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex]       uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### Gradle execution
[codex] 
[codex] To generate a dependency graph, the `dependency-submission` action must perform a Gradle execution that resolves
[codex] the dependencies of the project. All dependencies that are resolved in this execution will be included in the 
[codex] generated dependency graph.  By default action executes a built-in task that is designed to resolve all build dependencies
[codex] (`:ForceDependencyResolutionPlugin_resolveAllDependencies`).
[codex] 
[codex] The action looks for a Gradle project in the root of the workspace, and executes this project with
[codex] the Gradle wrapper, if configured for the project. If the wrapper is not configured, whatever `gradle` available
[codex] on the command-line will be used.
[codex] 
[codex] The action provides the ability to override the Gradle version and task to execute, as well as provide 
[codex] additional arguments that will be passed to Gradle on the command-line. See [Configuration Parameters](#configuration-parameters) below.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Selecting a cache provider
[codex] 
[codex] See [Selecting a cache provider](setup-gradle.md#selecting-a-cache-provider) for details on choosing between the `enhanced` (default) and `basic` cache providers via the `cache-provider` input.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Disabling caching
[codex] 
[codex] Caching is enabled by default. You can disable caching for the action as follows:
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] cache-disabled: true
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### Publishing a Develocity Build Scan® from your dependency submission workflow
[codex] 
[codex] You can automatically publish a free Develocity Build Scan on every run of `gradle/actions/dependency-submission`. 
[codex] Three input parameters are required, one to enable publishing and two more to accept the 
[codex] [Develocity terms of use](https://gradle.com/help/legal-terms-of-use).
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex]     - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex]       uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex]       with:
[codex]         build-scan-publish: true
[codex]         build-scan-terms-of-use-url: "https://gradle.com/help/legal-terms-of-use"
[codex]         build-scan-terms-of-use-agree: "yes"
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] A Build Scan makes it easy to determine the source of any dependency vulnerabilities in your project.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Configuration parameters
[codex] 
[codex] In some cases, the default action configuration will not be sufficient, and additional action parameters will need to be specified.
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex]     - name: Generate and save dependency graph
[codex]       uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex]       with:
[codex]         # Use a particular Gradle version instead of the configured wrapper.
[codex]         gradle-version: '8.6'
[codex] 
[codex]         # The gradle project is not in the root of the repository.
[codex]         build-root-directory: my-gradle-project
[codex] 
[codex]         # Choose a task that will trigger dependency resolution
[codex]         dependency-resolution-task: myDependencyResolutionTask
[codex] 
[codex]         # Additional arguments that should be passed to execute Gradle
[codex]         additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] 
[codex]         # Enable configuration-cache reuse for this build.
[codex]         cache-encryption-key: ${{ secrets.GRADLE_ENCRYPTION_KEY }}
[codex] 
[codex]         # Do not attempt to submit the dependency-graph. Save it as a workflow artifact.
[codex]         dependency-graph: generate-and-upload
[codex] 
[codex]         # Change the number of days that workflow artifacts are retained. (Default is 30 days).
[codex]         artifact-retention-days: 5
[codex] 
[codex]         # Specify the location where dependency graph files will be generated.
[codex]         dependency-graph-report-dir: custom-report-dir
[codex] 
[codex]         # By default, failure to generate a dependency graph will cause the workflow to fail
[codex]         dependency-graph-continue-on-failure: true
[codex] 
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] See the [Action Metadata file](../dependency-submission/action.yml) for a more detailed description of each input parameter.
[codex] 
[codex] The `GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin` can be further 
[codex] [configured via a number of environment variables](https://github.com/gradle/github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin?#required-environment-variables). 
[codex] These will be automatically set by the `dependency-submission` action, but you may override these values 
[codex] by setting them explicitly in your workflow file.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Reducing storage costs for saved dependency graph artifacts
[codex] 
[codex] By default, the dependency graph that is generated is stored as a workflow artifact.
[codex] To reduce storage costs for these artifacts, you can:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Set the `artifact-retention-days`:
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex]     - name: Generate dependency graph but only store workflow artifacts for 1 day
[codex]       uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex]       with:
[codex]         artifact-retention-days: 1 # Default is 30 days or as configured for repository
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] 2. Disable storing dependency-graph artifacts using `generate-and-submit`
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex]     - name: Generate and submit dependency graph but do not store as workflow artifact
[codex]       uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex]       with:
[codex]         dependency-graph: 'generate-and-submit' # Default value is 'generate-submit-and-upload'
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] # Resolving a dependency vulnerability
[codex] 
[codex] ## Finding the source of a dependency vulnerability
[codex] 
[codex] Once you have submitted a dependency graph, you may receive Dependabot Alerts warning about vulnerabilities in
[codex] dependencies of your project. In the case of transitive dependencies, it may not be obvious how that dependency is
[codex] used or what you can do to address the vulnerability alert.
[codex] 
[codex] The first step to investigating a Dependabot Alert is to determine the source of the dependency. One of the best ways to 
[codex] do so is with a free Develocity Build Scan®, which makes it easy to explore the dependencies resolved in your build.
[codex] 
[codex] &lt;img width="1069" alt="image" src="https://github.com/gradle/actions/assets/179734/3a637dfd-396c-4e94-8332-dcc6eb5a35ac">
[codex] 
[codex] In this example, we are searching for dependencies matching the name 'com.squareup.okio:okio' in the _Build Dependencies_ of 
[codex] the project. You can easily see that this dependency originates from 'com.github.ben-manes:gradle-versions-plugin'.
[codex] Knowing the source of the dependency can help determine how to deal with the Dependabot Alert.
[codex] 
[codex] Note that you may need to look at both the _Dependencies_ and the _Build Dependencies_ of your project to find the
[codex] offending dependency.
[codex] 
[codex] ### When you cannot publish a Build Scan®
[codex] 
[codex] If publishing a free Build Scan to https://scans.gradle.com isn't an option, and you don't have access to a private [Develocity
[codex] server](https://gradle.com/) for your project, you can obtain information about the each resolved dependency by running the `dependency-submission` workflow with debug logging enabled.
[codex] 
[codex] The simplest way to do so is to re-run the dependency-submission job with debug logging enabled:
[codex] 
[codex] &lt;img width="665" alt="image" src="https://github.com/gradle/actions/assets/179734/d95b889a-09fb-4731-91f2-baebbf647e31">
[codex] 
[codex] When you do so, the Gradle build that generates the dependency-graph will include a log message for each dependency version included in the graph.
[codex] Given the details in one log message, you can run (locally) the built-in [dependencyInsight](https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/viewing_debugging_dependencies.html#dependency_insights) task
[codex] to determine exactly how the dependency was resolved. 
[codex] 
[codex] For example, given the following message in the logs:
[codex] ```
[codex] Detected dependency 'com.google.guava:guava:32.1.3-jre': project = ':my-subproject', configuration = 'compileClasspath'
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] You would run the following command locally:
[codex] ```
[codex] ./gradlew :my-subproject:dependencyInsight --configuration compileClasspath --dependency com.google.guava:guava:32.1.3-jre
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] #### Dealing with 'classpath' configuration
[codex] 
[codex] If the configuration value in the log message is "classpath" then instead of running `dependency-insight` you'll need to run the Gradle
[codex] `buildEnvironment` task.
[codex] 
[codex] For example, given the following message in the logs:
[codex] ```
[codex] Detected dependency 'xerces:xercesImpl:2.12.2': project = ':my-subproject', configuration = 'classpath'
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] You would run the following command locally to expose the `xercesImpl` dependency:
[codex] ```
[codex] ./gradlew :my-subproject:buildEnvironment | grep -C 5 xercesImpl
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ## Updating the dependency version
[codex] 
[codex] Once you've discovered the source of the dependency, the most obvious fix is to update the dependency to a patched version that does not
[codex] suffer the vulnerability. For direct dependencies, this is often straightforward.  But for transitive dependencies it can be tricky.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Dependency source is specified directly in the build
[codex] 
[codex] If the dependency is used to compile your code or run your tests, it's normal for the underlying "source" of the dependency to have a
[codex] version configured directly in the build. For example, if you have a vulnerable version of `com.squareup.okio:okio` in your `compileClasspath`, then
[codex] it's likely you have a dependency like `com.squareup.moshi:moshi` configured as an `api` or `implementation` dependency.
[codex] 
[codex] In this case there are 2 possibilities:
[codex] 1. There is a newer, compatible version of `com.squareup.moshi:moshi` available, and you can just bump the version number.
[codex] 2. There isn't a newer, compatible version of `com.squareup.moshi:moshi`
[codex] 
[codex] In the second case, you can add a Dependency Constraint, to force the use of the newest version of `com.squareup.okio`:
[codex] 
[codex] ```kotlin
[codex] dependencies {
[codex]   implementation("com.squareup.moshi:moshi:1.12.0")
[codex]   constraints {
[codex]     // Force a newer version of okio in transitive resolution
[codex]     implementation("com.squareup.okio:okio:3.6.0")
[codex]   }
[codex] }
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### Dependency source is a plugin classpath
[codex] 
[codex] If the vulnerable dependency is introduced by a Gradle plugin, again the best option is to look for a newer version of the plugin.
[codex] But if none is available, you can still use a dependency constraint to force a newer transitive version to be used.
[codex] 
[codex] The dependency constraint must be added to the `classpath` configuration of the buildscript that loads the plugin.
[codex] 
[codex] ```kotlin
[codex] buildscript {
[codex]   repositories {
[codex]     gradlePluginPortal()
[codex]   }
[codex]   dependencies {
[codex]     constraints {
[codex]       // Force a newer version of okio in transitive resolution
[codex]       classpath("com.squareup.okio:okio:3.6.0")
[codex]     }
[codex]   }
[codex] }
[codex] plugins {
[codex]   id("com.github.ben-manes.versions") version("0.51.0")
[codex] }
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ## Limiting the dependencies that appear in the dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] By default, the `dependency-submission` action attempts to detect all dependencies declared and used by your Gradle build.
[codex] At times it may helpful to limit the dependencies reported to GitHub, to avoid security alerts for dependencies that 
[codex] don't form a critical part of your product. For example, a vulnerability in the tool you use to generate documentation 
[codex] may not be as important as a vulnerability in one of your runtime dependencies.
[codex] 
[codex] The `dependency-submission` action provides a convenient mechanism to filter the projects and configurations that
[codex] contribute to the dependency graph.
[codex] 
[codex] > [!NOTE]
[codex] > Ideally, all dependencies involved in building and testing a project will be extracted and reported in a dependency graph. 
[codex] > These dependencies would be assigned to different scopes (eg development, runtime, testing) and the GitHub UI would make it easy to opt-in to security alerts for different dependency scopes.
[codex] > However, this functionality does not yet exist.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Selecting Gradle projects that will contribute to the dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] If you do not want the dependency graph to include dependencies from every project in your build, 
[codex] you can easily exclude or include certain projects from the dependency extraction process.
[codex] 
[codex] To restrict which Gradle subprojects contribute to the report, specify which projects to exclude or include via a regular expression.
[codex] You can use the `dependency-graph-exclude-projects` and `dependency-graph-include-projects` input parameters for this purpose.
[codex] 
[codex] Note that excluding a project in this way only removes dependencies that are _resolved_ as part of that project, and may
[codex] not necessarily remove all dependencies _declared_ in that project. If another project depends on the excluded project
[codex] then it may transitively resolve dependencies declared in the excluded project: these dependencies will still be included
[codex] in the generated dependency graph.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Selecting Gradle configurations that will contribute to the dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] Similarly to Gradle projects, it is possible to exclude or include a set of dependency configurations from dependency graph generation,
[codex] so that only dependencies resolved by the included configurations are reported.
[codex] 
[codex] To restrict which Gradle configurations contribute to the report, specify which configurations to exclude or include via a regular expression.
[codex] You can use the `dependency-graph-exclude-configurations` and `dependency-graph-include-configurations` input parameters for this purpose.
[codex] 
[codex] Note that configuration exclusion applies to the configuration in which the dependency is _resolved_ which is not necessarily
[codex] the configuration where the dependency is _declared_. For example if you decare a dependency as `implementation` in
[codex] a Java project, that dependency will be resolved in `compileClasspath`, `runtimeClasspath` and possibly other configurations.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Example of project and configuration filtering
[codex] 
[codex] For example, if you want to exclude dependencies resolved by the `buildSrc` project, and exclude dependencies from the `testCompileClasspath` and `testRuntimeClasspath` configurations, you would use the following configuration:
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex]     - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex]       uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex]       with:
[codex]         # Exclude all dependencies that originate solely in the 'buildSrc' project
[codex]         dependency-graph-exclude-projects: ':buildSrc'
[codex]         # Exclude dependencies that are only resolved in test classpaths
[codex]         dependency-graph-exclude-configurations: '.*[Tt]est(Compile|Runtime)Classpath'
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] # Advance usage scenarios
[codex] 
[codex] ## Using a custom plugin repository
[codex] 
[codex] By default, the action downloads the `github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin` from the Gradle Plugin Portal (https://plugins.gradle.org). If your GitHub Actions environment does not have access to this URL, you can specify a custom plugin repository to use with an environment variable.
[codex] 
[codex] See [the setup-gradle docs](setup-gradle.md#using-a-custom-plugin-repository) for details.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Integrating the `dependency-review-action`
[codex] 
[codex] The GitHub [dependency-review-action](https://github.com/actions/dependency-review-action) helps you 
[codex] understand dependency changes (and the security impact of these changes) for a pull request,
[codex] by comparing the dependency graph for the pull-request with that of the HEAD commit.
[codex] 
[codex] Integrating the Dependency Review Action requires 2 changes to your workflows:
[codex] 
[codex] #### 1. Add a `pull_request` trigger to your existing Dependency Submission workflow.
[codex] 
[codex] In order to perform Dependency Review on a pull request, the dependency graph must be submitted for the pull request.
[codex] To do this, simply add a `pull_request` trigger to your existing dependency submission workflow.
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] name: Dependency Submission
[codex] 
[codex] on:
[codex]   push:
[codex]     branches: [ 'main' ]
[codex]   pull_request:
[codex] 
[codex] permissions:
[codex]   contents: write
[codex] 
[codex] diff --git a/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2c173270429b8ec6b75768a830961c534a59233e
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644

[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] failure triage are owned by
[codex] [orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Update discovery
[codex] 
[codex] `renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] 
[codex] `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] `basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] 
[codex] Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] `GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] `${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] `service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] as a clean security result.
[codex] 
[codex] The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Bot pull request checks
[codex] 
[codex] Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] require approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] ./gradlew clean build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] 
[codex] Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex]    App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex]    encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex]    Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex]    `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex]    `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex]    timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] 2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex]    `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex]    `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex]    `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex]    exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex]    accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, complete application
[codex]    runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] 3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex]    WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex]    Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex]    insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex]    infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] 4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex]    secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex]    Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] 5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex]    overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex]    disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex]    Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] 
[codex] The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] parity.
[codex] 
[codex] diff --git a/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..2c173270429b8ec6b75768a830961c534a59233e
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 7 credential-free extraction found 39 dependency occurrences across
[codex] +eight files: 19 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence,
[codex] +three Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three
[codex] +workflows. Public lookups returned 45 candidate branches before schedule,
[codex] +concurrency, and dashboard controls. The run extracted both internal
[codex] +coordinates but could not look them up, and Actions lookups reported that a
[codex] +GitHub token was required. Those authenticated lookups remain pending Phase 12.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result. A build step resolves and verifies the application
[codex] +and test trees before the official action performs its unfiltered graph pass;
[codex] +this prevents the graph task's zero exit status from masking unresolved
[codex] +configurations.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +The credential-free Phase 7 run generated an official local plugin snapshot,
[codex] +but it is deliberately recorded as incomplete. It contained 87 build/tool
[codex] +coordinates and zero `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring WebFlux, Spring Security,
[codex] +Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, or Lettuce coordinates. Its only Spring runtime
[codex] +names were `spring-core` and `spring-jcl`, alongside build-plugin Jackson
[codex] +coordinates. Gradle's `runtimeClasspath` report marked every application
[codex] +dependency failed, and
[codex] +`./gradlew dependencyInsight --dependency org.budgetanalyzer:service-web
[codex] +--configuration runtimeClasspath` identified HTTP 401 from GitHub Packages for
[codex] +`service-web` 0.0.16. No Netty coverage is available from that partial snapshot.
[codex] +This is authentication-dependent local evidence, not a submitted graph or a
[codex] +claim that those dependencies are absent.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, successful
[codex] +   `./gradlew --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache build`, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify application and test dependency resolution
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: ./gradlew --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache build
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 7 credential-free extraction found 39 dependency occurrences across
[codex] +eight files: 19 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence,
[codex] +three Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three
[codex] +workflows. Public lookups returned 45 candidate branches before schedule,

[codex] +coordinates but could not look them up, and Actions lookups reported that a

[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the

[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '43 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify application and test dependency resolution
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: ./gradlew --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache build
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,138 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] +version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 7 credential-free extraction found 39 dependency occurrences across
[codex] +eight files: 19 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence,
[codex] +three Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three
[codex] +workflows. Public lookups returned 45 candidate branches before schedule,
[codex] +concurrency, and dashboard controls. The run extracted both internal
[codex] +coordinates but could not look them up, and Actions lookups reported that a
[codex] +GitHub token was required. Those authenticated lookups remain pending Phase 12.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, Jackson, Lettuce, and other
[codex] +versions inherited through the Spring platform remain represented by the
[codex] +resolved dependency graph described below. A Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved reactive dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] +and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either declared
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be read, dependency resolution is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] +dependencies, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure
[codex] +as a clean security result. A build step resolves and verifies the application
[codex] +and test trees before the official action performs its unfiltered graph pass;
[codex] +this prevents the graph task's zero exit status from masking unresolved
[codex] +configurations.
[codex] +
[codex] +The accepted hosted graph must demonstrate the service's actual reactive tree,
[codex] +including Spring WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security,
[codex] +Spring Framework, and Jackson where resolved in application runtime or test
[codex] +configurations. Inspect Lettuce's Netty path as well as WebFlux's HTTP client and
[codex] +server path. Embedded Tomcat is not an expected dependency for this reactive
[codex] +service and must not be added as an artificial acceptance condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +The credential-free Phase 7 run generated an official local plugin snapshot,
[codex] +but it is deliberately recorded as incomplete. It contained 87 build/tool
[codex] +coordinates and zero `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring WebFlux, Spring Security,
[codex] +Reactor, Reactor Netty, Netty, or Lettuce coordinates. Its only Spring runtime
[codex] +names were `spring-core` and `spring-jcl`, alongside build-plugin Jackson
[codex] +coordinates. Gradle's `runtimeClasspath` report marked every application
[codex] +dependency failed, and
[codex] +`./gradlew dependencyInsight --dependency org.budgetanalyzer:service-web
[codex] +--configuration runtimeClasspath` identified HTTP 401 from GitHub Packages for
[codex] +`service-web` 0.0.16. No Netty coverage is available from that partial snapshot.
[codex] +This is authentication-dependent local evidence, not a submitted graph or a
[codex] +claim that those dependencies are absent.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, image ARM64
[codex] +support, and whether a proposed direct dependency actually remediates an
[codex] +inherited alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and
[codex] +require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 7 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, image-registry, or Mend
[codex] +credentials. The following `session-gateway` checks remain pending Phase 12:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant alert-read access, and configure its supported
[codex] +   encrypted Maven host rule. Retain the hosted Renovate log or Dependency
[codex] +   Dashboard URL proving successful preset resolution and lookups for Actions,
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform`, and
[codex] +   `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web`, with no authentication, configuration,
[codex] +   timeout, or lookup failures.
[codex] +2. From trusted `main`, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. Leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository secret store without
[codex] +   exposing their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL and
[codex] +   accepted GitHub snapshot proving both POM preflights, successful
[codex] +   `./gradlew --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache build`, complete application
[codex] +   runtime and test resolution, and graph submission with `${{ github.token }}`.
[codex] +3. In that accepted snapshot, retain package/configuration evidence for Spring
[codex] +   WebFlux, Reactor Core, Reactor Netty, Netty, Spring Security, Spring
[codex] +   Framework, Jackson, and Lettuce where actually resolved. Use Gradle dependency
[codex] +   insight to explain the inherited path and any missing expected family. Do not
[codex] +   infer remediation from a Boot or BOM update alone, and do not require Tomcat.
[codex] +4. Run the existing trusted build for the selected bot branch with package-read
[codex] +   secrets available and retain its URL. A historical build or a local
[codex] +   Maven-cached build is not evidence that the new workflow resolved remotely.
[codex] +5. Confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts are enabled while
[codex] +   overlapping Dependabot version-update and security-update pull requests remain
[codex] +   disabled. Retain alert URLs or explained misses for inherited reactive and
[codex] +   Spring dependencies after the complete graph is accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and explained misses there; local Phase 7
[codex] +preparation does not activate automation or establish vulnerability-review
[codex] +parity.

[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] failure triage are owned by
[codex] [orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] This document records only the `session-gateway` integration and review checks.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Update discovery
[codex]

[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] and workflow actions. The catalog extraction includes the `serviceCommon`
[codex] version shared by the declared `spring-platform` and `service-web` coordinates.
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] credentials and verify an authenticated lookup. Never put a package token in
[codex] this repository or the shared preset.
[codex]
